Then it wedged 19 km short of Centra Ruins, and that is a separate, older fault. **The position track names it outright: the hull ran back and forth along the SAME 1100-unit diagonal between (2996,36650) and (3653,35747), ~5 s a lap, for 90 s — and NO `bow blocked` was logged at the wedge, so it was never against terrain. It was steering itself in circles.** **Mechanism: the `aimClear` half of the Bug2 leave condition.** Wall-follow turns the hull away; from the new heading the aim is LOS-clear so the guard releases; the hull swings at the goal; the bow blocks; the guard re-engages and turns it away. **Nothing in that loop requires the hull to have got CLOSER, so it runs forever.** `aimClear` is a statement about geometry from where you stand; leaving a wall needs a statement about PROGRESS. **THE OBVIOUS FIX WAS IMPLEMENTED, MEASURED AND REJECTED** — demanding progress before any aim-clear release **regressed four routes** in the offline matrix, because in a tight coastal approach the guard then holds on past the berth. Recorded because it looks right and is not. **Shipped instead: arm the brake by EVIDENCE.** Count guard engagements that happen without the hull having got closer since the last one; only when **three** stack up call it a cycle, then flip the side and commit for **3 s with no early release**. A normal wall-follow never reaches the counter and is unchanged. Plus a **15 s wall-follow timeout** so no orbit is unbounded. Log: `wall-follow to the left (limit cycle -- reversing the side and committing)`. **Verified: offline matrix 26 destinations x 6 starts x 3 speed/turn combos = 450 runs, ZERO failures** (the rejected variant: 4 failures on the same matrix). The shipped C++ and `offline/gexec3.py` carry the identical rule, so the matrix tests what ships. Grid/berths/coordinates unchanged from `.65`; 26 ok / 0 bad; aboard_test passes. **BAT: drive Timber -> Centra Ruins, the leg that wedged.** If it hits the brake you will see the `limit cycle` line in the log. Worth also repeating FH and one long crossing to confirm nothing regressed. **STILL OPEN: the coordinate audit** — 19 catalog entries sit >600u from any wmsetus location record (Edea's House 6.4 km, White SeeD Ship 4.5 km, Shumi Village 4.1 km, Esthar City 7.1 km, Lunar Gate 9.1 km, Cactuar Island 17.6 km). FH proved that class of error is real and silent.

**Prior (v0.20.65 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: THE FH COORDINATE WAS WRONG BY 30 KM).** Aaron: *"You can drive Garden back to FH at any point after it becomes mobile"* — then he drove in by hand, and that log answered in one line what four builds could not: `Exited world map ... lastPos=(18895,-2122)` then `ChaseDetector: name debounce settled: id=0x025A name='fhdeck2'`. **The catalog had FH at (48811,-1653). The real FH is (20480,-2560) — wmsetus location record 13, 1644 units from where the transition fired.** The old point is terrain 7 on the Esthar west coast, not Garden-navigable, no disembark bit within 15 km; the real one is **terrain 28, a platform in OPEN OCEAN, Garden-navigable, with 122 Garden-disembark cells clustered around it.** **Every geometric conclusion from `.59` onward was correct ABOUT THE WRONG PLACE** — the 2273-unit standoff, the eight contact cells, the coastal wall gap, the railroad-side reasoning: all of it accurately describes a piece of Esthar coastline that has nothing to do with FH. **The `.64` trigger-table reading was ALSO sound and is kept** (the programs really do gate on vehicle/story/region, and there really is only one Garden clause) — it was simply fed a coordinate 30 km off. **FH's real segment (col 18, row 12) carries NO region byte, so no trigger program and no vehicle restriction: the engine let the Garden in at ~1650 units.** **THE LESSON, recorded in the source: applying a correct analysis to a wrong input is how you ship a confident wrong answer — validate the INPUT as hard as the analysis.** FH is now an ordinary berth at (20480,-2560), walk 0; **26/39 reachable**. Verified from 8 starts x 4 speed/turn combos: all arrive, 0-1 replans, and **every run crosses the ~1650u trigger radius before arrival**, so the field should load on approach. `drive_in`/nose-in/dock-site/patrol machinery retained but unused. **COORDINATE AUDIT: all 39 catalog entries cross-checked against the 64-record wmsetus location table — FH now matches exactly, but 19 sit >600u from any record.** Some may be legitimate (Alien Ship sidequest points, offshore islands may not be in that table) but **Edea's House 6.4 km, White SeeD Ship 4.5 km, Shumi Village 4.1 km, Esthar City 7.1 km, Lunar Gate 9.1 km, Cactuar Island 17.6 km deserve the same scrutiny FH just got** — logged, not touched. **BAT: drive the Garden to Fisherman's Horizon. Expect the field to load on approach, before the berth.** Then one ordinary coastal drive (Dollet / Shumi Village) to exercise the `.63` off-grid recovery.

**Prior (v0.20.64 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80 ANSWER FOUND IN THE GAME FILES: THERE IS NO GARDEN ENTRY FOR FISHERMAN'S HORIZON).** Aaron: *"Go back to the game exe and world map files and pin this down."* Right call — four builds went into geometry and the answer was never in the geometry. **The world-map field-entry programs (wmsetus.obj mod-section 8 @ offset 2928, decoded at v0.14.93, sitting in `src/world_map_trigger_data.inl` and already trusted by the foot planner) contain EXACTLY ONE clause in the whole 38-program table that names the Garden: program 20, locID 0x0172, topVeh `TRIG_VEH_GARDEN`, story 636..3899, clause region 0x0C.** Mapping the 32x24 segment-region byte map (mod section 2 @ offset 588, one byte per 8192u segment) onto the catalog: **region 0x0C is the northern sea whose only catalog destination is TRABIA GARDEN. FH is region 0x0A, and every clause on 0x0A is FOOT or FOOT_ALT.** No Garden clause for FH exists anywhere, so no amount of steering can fire one. **Four independent facts now agree:** no Garden clause in the trigger table; FH's platform carries no Garden disembark bit within 15 km; it DOES carry the Ragnarok landing bit; and the hull cannot come within 2273 units of the marker from any bearing, with all eight water/land contact cells now swept without a handoff. **The docking Aaron remembers is the scripted story event, not a repeatable trigger.** FH now gets its own honest catalog line (*"the world map has no Garden entry for it -- you reach it on foot or in the Ragnarok"*); reachable 26 -> 25. **NEW `[GARDEN] trigger state` log on boarding** — the live story word, whether program 20 is inside its 636..3899 window at all, and the hull's region byte — so this is checkable from a log instead of trusted. **If that line says EXPIRED, program 20 is dead at Aaron's story point and even Trabia Garden has no live Garden drive-in.** Also fixed the `.63` patrol, which set wantUp=false on a blocked bow and so spun on the spot for 82 s. Dock machinery kept and wired to Trabia Garden (the one real Garden clause) but NOT enabled — Trabia's berth already lands on the destination with 0 walk, so there is nothing to gain by risking it. Grid and berths unchanged; 25 ok / 0 bad; aboard_test passes (its `WmSafeReadBytes` stub now refuses unplanted addresses instead of dereferencing them). **BAT: board the Garden and read the `[GARDEN] trigger state` lines — that is the whole point of this build. Then try FH from the catalog and confirm the honest refusal, and drive one ordinary coastal destination (Dollet / Shumi Village) to exercise the .63 off-grid recovery.** **NEXT, if the story word says program 20 is live:** the one experiment left is driving the Garden into Trabia Garden to see whether a Garden field-entry clause fires at all — that would confirm the mechanism and close the model.

**Prior (v0.20.63 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: OFF-GRID RECOVERY; the hull can stand on a cell the planner calls blocked and be completely paralysed by it).** `.62` BAT gave **two results**. (1) **The wall gap is RULED OUT** — the run reached site 0 and swept it properly (`nose-in site 0 phase N` at (45643,507)/(45653,455)/(45539,811)/(45574,692), each within 60-80 units of the three contact cells) and nothing fired. Dropped from the table; the untried **south tip goes first now**. (2) **A real bug with nothing to do with FH**: on the way to site 1 the hull froze at `(-16605,-1050)` for 90 s — `bow blocked` on EVERY heading, `remain` unchanged across four replans, position constant. **Its FINE cell there is terrain 33 at height 0, open water; it is the 256 cell around it that is blocked, by a cliff in another corner.** The planner grid is deliberately conservative (a cell counts only if all four 128u sub-points do), so **every coastline carries a fringe of cells the engine lets the hull skim and the grid calls solid** — and once inside one, EVERY probe fails: `GdLineClear` rejects its first sample because that sample is still in the blocked cell, and `GdStepOpen` reads edge bits a blocked cell never carries. The wall guard then finds no clear heading anywhere and nothing moves. **There was no escape from that state at all.** **Fix: the executor checks its own cell first** — off-grid it stops asking the grid anything, steers at the nearest traversable cell centre with the guard bypassed, reverses after 6 s if that fails, and re-plans on return (`[GARDEN] OFF-GRID at` / `back on the grid at`). **This is general robustness, not an FH fix — any coastal berth could have produced it.** **Also new: the SHORELINE PATROL.** When every discrete contact point has been pressed, the run hugs the coast for 90 s aiming at the marker, tracing once a second. **If that finds nothing, there is no world-map proximity trigger for FH anywhere the hull can reach** — the eight-cell contact list plus a full coast patrol leaves nowhere else — **and the search moves into the world-map event scripts.** **world_garden.inl also breached the 80 KB CI hard fail (82,918 bytes) and is SPLIT** at the end of `Garden_BuildEnd` into `world_garden_grid.inl` (grid + build, 25.7 KB) and `world_garden.inl` (everything that reads the grid, 58.6 KB); no code changed in the move, `world_map.cpp` includes grid first, both harnesses reproduce the same totals. Verified: south tip approach 35.6 km direct, drivable 6 starts x 4 combos, zero failures; grid/berths/harnesses unchanged (26 ok / 0 bad, aboard_test all pass). **BAT: drive to Fisherman's Horizon and let it run to the end** — south tip sweep, then "Searching along the shore", then either a dock or the honest "Searched the whole shore". **ALSO worth one ordinary drive somewhere coastal (Dollet or Shumi Village) to exercise the off-grid recovery**, since that is the change most likely to matter elsewhere. **QUESTION OUT TO AARON:** whether the FH docking is available at his point in the story at all, and whether re-docking after the FH story beat is a thing the game still offers — if it is not, no amount of driving will ever trigger it and FH should be re-labelled in the catalog rather than chased.

**Prior (v0.20.62 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: stop betting on one dock site; ask the walkmesh which points of FH the hull can actually TOUCH).** `.61` BAT: *"Still failed to reach FH."* **The drive was CLEAN** — 107 waypoints, direct 35 km route, approach reached in 19 s, no cliff wedge, `[GARDEN] cliff gate: 671236 east edges and 669555 south edges open` exactly as predicted. **The `.61` grid fix holds.** What was wrong was the press point: the screenshots show the hull grinding against a bare grey **terrain-29 cliff face** with the rail bridge marching out over the ocean behind it. No city, nothing to dock with. **The question I should have asked two builds ago is not "how close to the marker can the hull get" but "which points of FH can the hull TOUCH".** Take FH's foot landmass (the label containing the marker — **1,842 cells, its OWN component, not Esthar's**) and list every Garden-water cell on the map directly adjacent to it with no wall cell between: **exactly EIGHT, in two clusters** — the **wall gap** `(45632,576)/(45632,704)/(45760,320)`, 3.6-4.0 km from the marker on the FAR side of the rail bridge, and the **south tip** `(48832,-7360)...(49472,-8128)`, 5.7-6.5 km on the Balamb side. **Nothing else on the map touches FH at all, and the `.61` press point `(46850,-2970)` is in neither cluster** — it could never have worked. **`.60` picked the gap correctly and NEVER REACHED IT** (the cliff bug wedged the hull 50 km short), so the gap has still never been tried, and the "120 km detour" `.61` rejected it for is **~70 seconds** at the hull's real speed (the `.60` log clocks 30 km in 17 s) — a bad reason. **Fix: `s_gardenDocks[]` lists EVERY touchable site per drive_in destination and the run works through them** — sweep site 0, and if nothing fires drive to site 1 and sweep that (*"No dock here. Trying the other side."*) instead of reporting failure. Nose-in now traces the hull once a second, so a sweep that finds nothing is still evidence. **Verified:** both approaches drivable from 5 starts x 3 combos (site 0 route 120 km, site 1 36 km); engine-grid sim puts the sweep **within 15-65 units of the real contact cells at both sites**, and site 1 covers all five of its contacts. Grid/berths/harnesses unchanged from `.61` (26 ok / 0 bad, aboard_test all pass). **BAT: drive to Fisherman's Horizon and let it run — it will sail around the rail bridge (about a minute), sweep, and if that fails announce "Trying the other side" and sweep the south tip. Either it docks or we get the honest "tried every point on its shore the hull can reach".** In the latter case grep `[GARDEN] nose-in trace` — **if both sites come up empty the docking is not a world-map proximity trigger at all, the eight-cell list leaves it nowhere to hide, and the search moves into the world-map event scripts.**

**Prior (v0.20.61 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: the 800 gate is right for a RAMP and wrong for a CLIFF).** `.60` BAT: *"Got stuck ~50 km away."* **The F11 screenshots cracked it in seconds — the hull was not adrift, it was pressed against a two-tier CLIFF COASTLINE with the rail line along the top**, at `(-5127,-5196)` on the Galbadia east coast; the log has it holding UP for 90 s across nine stall/replans without moving 400 units. Those coastal polys **do** carry the Garden mask; what stops the hull is the 200 gate, climbing 227 units from sea level in ONE move step. **The `.57` derivation (256u cell = four 64u steps ⇒ budget 800) is correct for a ramp, whose drop accumulates across the cell, and wrong for a cliff, where the whole drop is one step however you quantise — and averaging four sub-heights into one cell height ERASES the cliff.** That is how a course got planned into a coastline. **Fix: apply the gate where the cliff is still visible — between the 128u SUB-POINTS the rasterizer already samples, at the engine's own 200.** Cell traversable only if none of its four internal sub-pairs is a cliff; edge open only if at least ONE of the two sub-point crossings is climbable (new bits `GDC_OPEN_E`/`GDC_OPEN_S`, honoured by the flood, the A* and `GdLineClear`; diagonals need one of their two L routes). **This satisfies BOTH BATs and nothing else does** — at the Centra shelf every sub-pair is far under 200 so the `.56` wedge does not return; at the Galbadia cliff they are 227/249/251 so the edge closes. Map-wide it shuts **14,401 of 5,466,034 sub-edges (0.26%)**; 678,223 cells traversable, 665,412 reachable, and **C++ and the independent Python model agree cell-for-cell on all three**. **Whole berth table re-derived and re-validated: 25 park-and-walk berths, each driven from 8 starts x 4 speed/turn combos, ZERO failures** (Balamb Town 384→256, Trabia 128→0, Tomb 256→286, Edea's House 724→1159). Reachable set unchanged at 26/39. **FH back on the BALAMB side:** `.60` read Aaron's railroad warning as a diagnosis and moved the approach past the far end of the rail bridge — the only wall gap is there, but reaching it is a **120 km route for a place 35 km away**, and the wedge happened en route. From the Balamb side it is 35 km direct, 0 replans; the press point is the FH marker and the hull comes to rest in the cove at ~`(46850,-2970)`, **2273 units off it, the closest the Garden mask allows from ANY bearing on the map**. `.60` never actually pressed — it reported honestly from 3204 units out — so **if the docking trigger is proximity anywhere in the 2300..3200 band, this reaches it**. **BAT: drive to Fisherman's Horizon, and drive one long crossing that used to hug a coast (Balamb → Centra Ruins or Winhill → Balamb Town) to confirm no cliff wedge.** Expect `[GARDEN] cliff gate: 671236 east edges and 669555 south edges open` at grid build. **NOTE the foot side still uses 800 and is deliberately untouched** — 200 at 128u over-blocked it in `.58` (severed Edea's House) and there is no finer foot data to sub-sample; **Chocobo Forest 4 / the Esthar coast merge is still open and is now the only place that model is suspect.**

**Prior (v0.20.60 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: the FH dock is a GAP IN THE COASTAL WALL).** `.59` BAT: *"Failed to dock at FH... Garden needs to steer into the FH model, not pull up alongside it - drive right into it. Also, make sure Garden is on the correct side of the transcontinental railroad."* **Both constraints are one geometric fact.** `.59` pressed at FH's location marker `(48790,-1788)`, which sits **2.6 km inside ground the Garden mask never permits** — the nearest Garden-navigable water to it on ANY bearing is 2560 units, so that press could never have worked from anywhere. And the transcontinental rail bridge (terrain 27) **severs the ocean in a solid 512-unit band at z ~ -1536..-1920**; the `.59` approach `(46016,-3392)` was SOUTH of it, the dock is north — exactly Aaron's warning. **The data names the dock unambiguously:** an adjacency scan of every Garden-water cell within 7.7 km of the marker, for water touching FH's landmass with no wall cell between, returns **exactly one place** — a ~450-unit gap at x~45700, z~+320..+770 (contacts at z=320/576/704). Every other cell of that coast is terrain 29, the Esthar mountain wall. One opening in a continuous wall, at the one destination you are told to drive INTO. **Fix:** `GardenPark` gains `dock_x`/`dock_y` (press point, named separately from the marker; zero = use the marker, so no other row changes meaning); FH approach `(46016,-3392) -> (45440,640)`, dock point `(46400,560)` straight east through the gap, `walk_units` 0. **The NOSE-IN phase now backs off between sweep positions** — simulated on the engine grid a JAMMED HULL CANNOT RE-AIM, and without a back-off phases 1..6 moved it zero units and re-pressed the identical spot; each phase after the first now reverses 900 ms first. **Verified:** approach reached 28/28 (7 starts x 4 speed/turn combos), 0 replans, worst error 283u; nose-in sim presses the shore at z = 618, 718, 502, 861, 330, covering all three contact cells; `gridtest` 26 ok / 0 bad; `aboard_test` ALL CHECKS PASSED. **BAT: drive to Fisherman's Horizon.** Either *"Docked at Fisherman's Horizon."* or the honest *"could not dock... stopped alongside it"* — in the latter case grep `[GARDEN] nose-in phase` for the exact points tried, which tells us whether the trigger is proximity-based at all. **Also still open — Chocobo Forest 4** and whether the 800 foot gate over-merges the Esthar coast.

**Prior (v0.20.59 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80: FH is a DOCKING destination).** Aaron: *"You dock the Garden at FH. Driving the Garden into FH triggers the docking."* That explains what `.58` could not: **FH's whole platform carries the foot bit and NEVER the disembark bit `byte15 & 0x02`** — not one such cell within 15 km. Not missing data: you don't step off there, so nothing is marked. **The bit's meaning is now corroborated map-wide — every landmass with NO disembark bit is one you can't step onto from a vehicle, and all of them carry the Ragnarok LANDING bit (`byte14 0x80`) instead: FH, Cactuar Island, both Islands Closest to Heaven/Hell, the Great Salt Lake shore.** New `drive_in` class: park_x/y is an APPROACH point, arrival is the world map handing off to a field (`Poll`'s exit edge asks `Garden_LeavingIsArrival()` first), and reaching the approach point with nothing fired reports honestly instead of faking an arrival. Approach (46016,-3392) chosen by the same validate-by-sim pass as the berths (the two closer candidates wedge 8/28 and 7/28). **26/39 reachable.** NOTE the docking is NOT in the decoded wmsetus §8: its only Garden clause is program 20 locID `0x0172`, and **field 370 is Lunatic Pandora, not FH** — so either the docking lives outside §8 or locID numbering ≠ field-id numbering. Recorded so nobody repeats that search. **OPEN — Chocobo Forest 4:** at the 200 foot gate it is its own 25,999-cell landmass; at the faithful 800 it MERGES into the 134,828-cell Esthar mass, and the merge is what excludes it. Aaron says the Esthar continent is walled by mountains you can't cross on foot either, so 800 is over-merging somewhere on that coast — worth a look before trusting any Esthar-adjacent verdict. **BAT: drive to Fisherman's Horizon — either "Docked at Fisherman's Horizon." or the honest "as close as it gets" line, which tells us where the approach point needs to be.**

**Prior (v0.20.58 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80 berth model corrected; Aaron caught it).** `.57` reported Shumi Village, Edea's House and Fisherman's Horizon as Ragnarok-only. **Two errors.** (1) **The SAME 4x gate mistake as `.57`, applied to the FOOT side** — foot components used 200 between 128u cells, but the foot move step is `0x20` = 32u so a 128 cell is four steps and the budget is **800**. At 200 it severed real landmasses (Edea's House cut off from Chocobo Forest 7's). Fixing the Garden side and not the foot side was an oversight: it is ONE derivation and applies to every vehicle. (2) **A berth is a PAIR of cells, not one.** The old rule wanted one cell to be Garden-traversable AND carry the disembark bit; physically the hull stops on water and the player steps off onto adjacent ground. **Shumi Village's own cell carries the disembark bit and is NOT Garden-traversable** — the old rule could never see it at any gate. Berth = hull cell + step-off cell (bit 0x02, foot, destination's landmass) within 384u, 6 km walk cap. **The table is now VALIDATED BY CONSTRUCTION: every candidate is simulated from 7 starts x 4 speed/turn combos and the first drivable one ships** (3 needed a fallback: Chocobo Forest 5, Shumi Village, Alien Ship 1). **25/39 reachable and the unreachable set is exactly Aaron's description** — the Esthar continent behind its mountain wall, the offshore islands, and DSRC. **Walks collapse:** Dollet 3415→1336, Galbadia Station 4891→0, G-Garden 4529→0, Choco 5 3238→1669; 14 of 25 berths are now within 400u and 9 are on top of the destination. Matrix **200/200**. **OPEN — Fisherman's Horizon:** still reports unreachable, and NOT for either reason above — there is **no disembark-bit cell within 15 km of FH**, its whole platform carries only the foot bit, so no rule built on that bit can see it; loosening to "any foot ground beside Garden water" re-admits the Esthar coast and contradicts the mountain wall. **Ask Aaron how you actually step off the Garden at FH.** **BAT: re-drive Shumi Village and Edea's House (both were refused before), plus Galbadia Station whose berth is now on top of the destination.**

**Prior (v0.20.57 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80 fix from the .56 BAT: the CONTINENTAL SHELF was reading as a wall).** Centra Ruins drive oscillated between (2885,36904) and (3920,35354) five times, logging `bow blocked` continuously **in open ocean**, `remain` stuck at ~27,000. **Two quantisation errors.** (1) `GdProbe` compared the hull's 256u cell to the cell one WHOLE 640u probe away and applied the 200 gate to that jump — a 640u probe spans 2.5 cells, so any slope longer than the probe accumulates past 200 though every cell step is legal. At the wedge the per-cell steps were 0/107/166/85/48/62/32/17 and the 2.5-cell jump 299. **All 15 logged `bow blocked` positions reproduce exactly** under that rule (`offline/garden_probe_repro.py`). `GdProbe` is now `!GdLineClear()`, which already marches the gate. (2) **The gate is 4x too strict at cell scale**: the engine tests |dH| >= 200 between polys ONE MOVE STEP apart and the Garden step is `0x40` = 64u, so a 256u cell = FOUR steps and the faithful budget is **800**. Map-wide, 200 blocks 2,955 edges + 3,990 cells; 800 blocks **ZERO edges** and one degenerate cell — the Garden's permission is the MASK (byte15 bit 0x20), not a slope rule. **Berths improved as a by-product: Galbadia Garden 4529 -> 0 units of walking, Galbadia Station 4891 -> 362.** Reachability unchanged (23/39). **METHODOLOGY FIX: `offline/garden_exec.py` probed the 128u grid, not the 256u grid the shipped code reads — "finer" is not "stricter", it is DIFFERENT, and it is why the .56 matrix missed this. `offline/garden_exec2.py` is fidelity-matched.** Full matrix now **184/184 pairs, was 175/184**; the BAT's own route (Winhill->Centra) goes from 7 replans / 2047u off to 0 replans / 257u. **Unreachable-by-design, confirmed:** Shumi Village, Edea's House, Fisherman's Horizon and 13 others sit on landmasses with ZERO Garden-parkable ground, and in every case the nearest berth is on a DIFFERENT landmass so it isn't walkable either — Ragnarok destinations. **BAT: re-drive Winhill -> Centra Ruins (the leg that failed) — expect a clean crossing with no `bow blocked` in open water. Also re-drive Galbadia Garden, whose berth moved onto the destination.**

**Prior (v0.20.56 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80 fix from the .55 BAT: you disembark the mobile Garden WITHOUT leaving the world map).** `.55` BAT: Timber drive parked fine, then after hopping off he heard "car" x3 and "Garden" x1 and the town was never entered. **Two independent faults.** (1) The `.55` aboard latch cleared only at a world-map ENTRY — and there ISN'T one when you step off the hull. Log has no entry between the park at 13:24:28 and walking away; backslash at 816u from the berth started a SECOND Garden drive. **Fix: continuous disembark detection that does NOT use the savemap mirror** (its refresh rate is still unproven) — while a Garden drive runs or the engine names the Garden in motion, the live position IS the hull, so record it; a sustained 450u separation from that recorded spot afterwards (700ms hold) = climbed out. Entry classification still runs and still wins when available. (2) **`CheckVehicleChange` committed locomotion 3/33/36/39/48 in three seconds while merely WALKING** — announcing the names AND repointing `GetWorldMapPosition_Active` at those savemap mirrors (the car's is 50 km away at the Missile Base; only the `.217` foot-motion override kept position sane, and only while moving). DEVNOTES has called that byte non-authoritative since `.255` but the commit path still trusted it outright. **Non-foot values now need corroboration** — the engine in-motion id naming the same family, or that vehicle's mirror within 600u of the player — else `[VEH-REJECT]`. Also skipped entirely during a Garden drive (the foot/car drive already had that exemption). Smaller: stall detector doesn't arm in the first 2.5s (the Timber run tripped it 3s in at its own start coord; the spurious replan widened arrival and cost 500u of berth accuracy), already-parked prompt 700→1500u. **`tests/garden_aboard_test.cpp` now replays BOTH BATs verbatim — 13 checks, all pass under g++.** **BAT: repeat Timber. After parking, step off and walk a few paces — expect `[GARDEN] disembark:` and NO vehicle announcements (any that appear should have a matching `[VEH-REJECT]`). Then backslash should give `[DRIVE] Start`, not `[GARDEN] start`. Also try boarding and pressing backslash WITHOUT moving first — that's the one path still resting on the entry classification.**

**Prior (v0.20.55 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built — #80 fix from the .54 BAT: the engine vehicle id is a VEHICLE-IN-MOTION register).** `.54` BAT: catalog never showed the Garden set, everything said "not reachable", and the post-park backslash started a foot drive. **All three are one wrong assumption.** `[0x020409E0]` reads **0** at every world-map entry and every catalog build and **48 exactly once — four seconds INTO an active drive** (13:08:51 `[VEHID]`), then 0 again while parked. It names a vehicle only while one is MOVING. `.54` asked it at the two moments it is always 0. **Fix: latch, don't sample.** A read of `0x30` is a one-way confirmation; the latch clears only on positive on-foot evidence at a world-map transition, where the savemap mirrors ARE fresh (13:10:34, just disembarked: `|P-char|=0`, `|P-bgu|=1015`) — three-way test (clear / set / **leave alone**) so an ambiguous entry never flips it. Aboard transition forces `s_catalogBuilt=false` and speaks "Piloting Balamb Garden."; `Garden_CellReachable` builds its flood on demand instead of answering false from an empty map; backslash at a berth you're already at says "leave the Garden first"; arrival 384→288 (the .54 park stopped 379u short, turning Fire Cavern's 362u walk into 666u). **`tests/garden_aboard_test.cpp` replays the BAT id sequence + real mirror values as a regression guard (g++, host-compilable, ALL CHECKS PASSED).** NOTE `[VEHPOS32]` never appeared in the .54 log — the locomotion byte never commits to 48, which is expected and is why nothing depends on it now. **BAT: listen for "Piloting Balamb Garden." right after the world map appears (or on first nudge); catalog should be long with the Esthar-side entries saying the Garden can't reach them; at the Fire Cavern berth press backslash BEFORE getting off (expect "Already parked... leave the Garden"), then disembark and press again for the walk-in. Grep [GARDEN] entry classify / aboard / catalog:.**

**Prior (v0.20.54 LOCAL, NOT MSVC-built / NOT BAT'd — #80 mobile Balamb Garden auto-drive).** New self-contained subsystem `src/world_garden.inl` (48 KB): its own 256u traversability grid, its own clearance-weighted A*, its own executor, gated on `GetActiveVehicleId() == 0x30` so nothing runs unless the player is piloting the Garden — the BAT-proven foot/car drive is untouched. **Engine facts read out of FF8_EN.exe this session (primary source, addresses in the file header):** `0x53E6B0 IsPolyWalkable(triple, veh)` is the whole per-vehicle table — foot `byte15&0x80`, car `0x40`, **Garden `0x20`**, chocobo `0x10`, Ragnarok always; `0x53E730` (via `0x54B860`) is the SET-DOWN table — car `0x04`, **Garden `0x02`**, chocobo `0x01`, **Ragnarok `byte14&0x80`**; the 200u step gate is NOT vehicle-dependent. Cross-checked over all 473,193 wmx polys: bit `0x40` is 0-1.1% on forest (re-derives cars-cannot-enter-forest), bit `0x20` is 99% on ocean / 13% on terrain 29 (the Garden sails, mountains stop it), `byte14 0x80` is 0% on ocean+mountain+forest and 97-100% on plains (an airship's landing rule). **23 of 39 destinations have a park point;** the other 16 are the Esthar continent + offshore islands = the engine's own "you need the Ragnarok". Park-and-walk is FORCED: only trigger program 20 (locID 0x0172, region 0x0C) has a Garden clause, so no destination can be driven into. Catalog keeps unreachable entries (announced as such) rather than hiding them; on foot the catalog gains **"Mobile Balamb Garden"** at the hull's live position. **Latent bug fixed on the way:** savemap vehicle mirrors are int32 X / int32 Y / int16 Z / int16 rot, NOT six int16 — a live `[VEHDUMP]` `car_pos` decodes to (-71100,-14815) (Missile Base coast) as int32 and (-6076,...) as int16; the old reader was wrong everywhere |X| > 32767, i.e. all of Galbadia/Esthar. `[VEHPOS32]` logs both. **Offline: C++ and Python grids agree cell-for-cell (WALK 677,934, PARK 65,035, zero disagreements)** — that validates the mesh transform `mz = 196608 - vwy`. Executor simulated on the 128u grid (finer than the planner's) from 8 starts x 4 speed/turn combos: **175/184 pairs, 95.1%**; the 9 failures are final-approach geometry into coastal berths, fail safe, and are listed in `offline/GARDEN_MATRIX.md`. **NOTE for the foot drive: the wall-guard fix in world_garden.inl is the #100/H2 limit cycle** — the existing `world_map_drive_exec.inl` rule turns toward the target side and never moves, which oscillates +-1 turn step against a wall forever. Porting it back is a separate, deliberate change. **BAT: board the Garden -> world map. Confirm `[GARDEN] aboard vehicleId=48` FIRST (this single gate is the biggest unknown). Then cycle the catalog, drive somewhere near (Balamb Town / Fire Cavern), leave the Garden, press backslash again to walk in. Then a long crossing (Deling City / Trabia) and one refusal (Esthar City). Send ff8_world.log; grep [GARDEN] and [VEHPOS32].** Docs: `Plan & Research Documents/Balamb Garden Auto-Drive - offline analysis.md`, `offline/GARDEN_README.md`, harness `tests/garden_harness.cpp`.

**Prior (v0.18.3.309 LOCAL, NOT pushed/BAT'd — #114 pin-escape + #110 RAWCAM diagnostic).** `.308` BAT (Aaron, manual): reached floor 3, "better at actually going down the stairs" but "still gets hung up quite a bit." Log root cause: after .308 fixed WHICH edge the nudge uses, the player still pins at moveDist=0 on rail/knee-wall corners and the recovery REPEATED the same nudge (`tri 9->8 dir=(-0.25,-0.97)` at rec 2/4/6). Offline geometry: the walkmesh path there is clear → the obstacle is COLLISION the walkmesh lacks (rails/hole wall). **#114 fix:** when the player hasn't moved since the last nudge, recovery ROTATES the escape (opposite perp / triangle centroid / back along corridor) and lengthens the nudge 8→32 ticks; resets on real movement. Pin statics in `field_nav_autodrive_helpers.inl`, cleared at drive start; log shows `escapeMode`/`pinned`. **#110 RAWCAM (passive):** disassembly this session established field movement = integer/table math (no FPU trig; sine LUT 0x479950), entity struct (pos +0x190/+0x194, facing candidates +0x08/0x0A/0x0C, tri +0x1FA, stride 0x264), controllable-idx 0x1CE475C, entity table 0x1DCB340. **Camera-0 `.ca` does NOT reproduce measured motion** on gpbig1a/gpbig2a (both `num_cameras=1` → NOT multi-camera). `FIELD_CAM_DIAG` logs per-frame raw pos/delta/tri/facing + a `.ca` dump so we can derive the true transform. Full RE notes: `FIELD_CAMERA_RE_FINDINGS.md`. **BAT diag: gpbig1a then gpbig2a, walk each arrow ~1s on OPEN ground, send ff8_field.log, grep [RAWCAM].** Auto-drive pin-fix BAT'able same session (diag is passive).

**Prior (v0.18.3.308 LOCAL — #113, the recovery nudge was perpendicular to the WRONG EDGE).** `neighbor[e]` pairs with edge **(v[e], v[e+1])** — the v0.17.9.14 FindPortal convention — but the even-phase recovery nudge still used the old **(e+1, e+2)** pair, so every nudge was perpendicular to the wrong segment (often a wall). **Log-proof, no inference:** the .306 wedge's 20 identical `tri 72->71 dir=(0.90,-0.44)` nudges == the old-rule output exactly; the true shared edge `(-1433,-1590)-(-781,-1402)` gives `(-0.28,0.96)` → points at tri 71. Convention check across gpbig1a+gpbig2a: old pair fails shared-vertex on **184/186** links, fixed pair **0/370**. This closes #100's third part (part 1 = #110 axes, part 2 = #112 goal instability). Also corrected the same derivation in the two DISABLED blocks (corridor steering, wall-bias) so re-enabling can't resurrect it, and in `field_nav_settriangle.inl`'s COORD diagnostic (log-only). **NEW OFFLINE HARNESS (this session):** g++-compiles the REAL `field_nav_pathfinding.inl` against the real gpbig1a walkmesh extract and simulates the drive loop (wall-sliding kinematic player); it reproduces .306's goal flip-flop (20/1/22 vs stable 48) and under .307+.308 **all 84 triangle-center starts reach the stairs line**. Point-player sim ≠ engine collision radius — machine BAT stays ground truth. **CONTEXT: Claude is running the BAT loop via computer use this session (Aaron away); .308 authored before .307 was machine-tested, so .308 is the first build actually BAT'd — log attribution stays clean (goal lines vs NOT-reset lines vs nudge-direction lines).** **BAT: calibrate (~2 steps) then drive to the stairs; in any wedge expect VARIED, corridor-pointing nudge directions; .307 expectations still hold (same [A*] goal every re-path, honest Stuck bound).**

**Prior (v0.18.3.307 LOCAL, NOT machine-BAT'd — #112 + the #100 half, ONE change: recovery must CONVERGE).** `.306` BAT: **#111 CONFIRMED — zero `Player position lost` all session.** Aaron: *"navigation really struggled to actually get around... stuck on objects, like the rails around the stairs and the short wall around the hole in the middle of the floor."* The log splits that into TWO faults. **Fault 1 (pre-existing, #110, deliberately untouched):** every gpbig1a drive before the 15:11:44 calibration ran on ca-quantized axes the calibration itself proves were 90° wrong (`camRight (1,0)->(0,1)`; second `[CAM-SNAP-VERDICT] ALTERNATIVE CORRECT` — but still gpbig1a, still consistency not independence). Wrong axes = keys rotated a quarter turn = the drive grinding INTO the rails/knee wall: drive 1 ran EAST while the stairs were SW (dist 1705→1948 while "driving"); drive 4 pressed UL into the wall for 27 s, moveDist=0, **20 IDENTICAL nudges** (`tri 72->71 dir=(0.90,-0.44)` — the #100 signature). Control group: the SAME build drove gpbig2a (flipBand=0, axes right at load) cleanly through 6 wp to the Bottom-crossing exit. The .305 cache cannot help a session's FIRST load (dies with the process); the threshold change stays gated on `gpgmn3`/`gpbrdg1`/`gppark1`. **Fault 2 (NEW = #112, fixed here):** both post-calibration drives had right axes and STILL failed — the final drive ping-ponged tri 8↔9↔10 for 40 s. Mechanism, read straight off the goals: for trigger targets the per-tick **"+300 past the line" offset moves WITH the player**, and the odd-phase/nudge-fallback recovery re-paths seeded `FindNearestTriangle()` with it → the A* goal depended on where the player stood. Drive start & wiggle-completion agree on raw-center goal **48**; recoveries produced **20/1/22** — goal-1's wp `(-2361,-503)` pulls WEST, goal-22's wp `(-1683,69)` pulls NORTH-EAST, so each recovery routed the player toward the opposite cluster, forever ("check ALL passes writing the same field", caught again). **Fix A: capture `rawTx/rawTz` before the offset; every recovery re-path seeds A*+funnel with it.** **Fix B (inseparable — it is what makes "Stuck." reachable, the #100 half):** every ping-pong hop changed triangle, and the v0.17.6.1 tri-advance reset refilled the budget on each hop → `phase=1` on EVERY recovery of the final drive, `MAX_RECOVERY_PHASES` unreachable, silent bouncing until the 40 s timeout. New **4-entry ring of recently-stuck triangles** (statics in `field_nav_autodrive_helpers.inl`, cleared at drive start in `handlekeys`): a fresh triangle still resets, a revisit logs `recovery counter NOT reset` and lets the phase climb to the cap → honest **"Stuck. Distance remaining: N."** Waypoint advances keep their own independent reset, so genuine corridors keep their budget. Nothing landed in `field_navigation.cpp` (still 81,792/81,920 — #37). **BAT: on gpbig1a walk a couple of steps FIRST until "Camera calibrated." (~2 steps today), THEN F9 → Stairs down to Floor 5, from two different spots.** Expect every recovery `[A*]` line to show the SAME goal, `NOT reset` lines where it used to ping-pong, and either the stairs or an honest Stuck within ~40 s. A Stuck log (stable goal + pin location) is exactly the evidence the next change needs — likely the stair-mouth approach geometry. Steps on `gpgmn3`/`gpbrdg1`/`gppark1` give #110 its independent verdict.

**Prior (v0.18.3.306 — BAT'd, #111 CONFIRMED).** **Auto-drive no longer dies on a ONE-FRAME player-position dropout.** `.305` BAT had 11 `Player position lost` stops in ~4 min; the log proved every one a SINGLE-FRAME dropout (position readable the tick before and after) — the engine re-resolving walkmesh placement racing our per-frame poll. ## Architecture, safety & workflow rules

- Read **`DEVNOTES.md`** + **`NEXT_SESSION_PROMPT.md`** at session start; `DEVNOTES_HISTORY.md` only when tracing past decisions. Update both at every version bump AND after every BAT.
- **Version bump = ONE place:** `FF8OPC_VERSION` in `src/ff8_accessibility.h`, paired with a new top `CHANGELOG.md` entry; `push_to_github.ps1` refuses if the top `## vX.Y.Z` ≠ the macro.
- **Filesystem MCP for Windows project files.** `filesystem:edit_file` CORRUPTS on a literal `$` in the replacement — use hex `0x24` or `write_file`. OneDrive throws transient EPERM on rename — retry once. (Cowork Read/Edit/Write also work; its bash sandbox mounts the project, so grepping logs there is fine.)
- **NEVER re-enable the SET3 hook (0x1E)** — interception hangs the infirmary scene. CI-guarded.
- **Victory/transition TTS hooks the text renderer, NOT memory.**
- **F-key handlers gated on `!(GetAsyncKeyState(VK_MENU) & 0x8000)`** (no Alt combos). **F12 = per-session diagnostic ONLY** — grep `VK_F12` and remove old code before hooking a new one.
- **Source size (CI guard):** 60 KB soft / 80 KB hard. Split via textual `#include` of `.inl` from the parent `.cpp` (no header guards/namespaces inside `.inl`; `*_state.inl` statics first). **Split BEFORE the edit that needs the room, not by shaving comments to fit** (`.286`–`.293` deleted explanation until `.294` had to split anyway). **Prove a split inert**: reconstruct the pre-split file from the fragment, build both, diff real output. **NEXT UP: `field_navigation.cpp` at 81,792/81,920 — split it before anything else lands there.**
- **Diagnostics:** gate behind `#define X 0`, don't delete.
- **DO NOT "fix" the 126 placeholder field names.** `FIELD_DISPLAY_NAMES` has 126 entries marked `(???)` whose display name is the raw archive filename ("bgcrash1", "bgdin_1"), and the mod reads them out. **Aaron has ruled on this, 2026-08-16: leave them.** *"They are better than nothing and inform the blind player the scene / field / background / camera changed."* A field name is a change cue first and a place name second. **`NO_NAME_FIELDS` stays a two-entry exception list, not a policy:** field 95 `testbl6` is a Try-again MENU, and field 152 `bgbtl_1` is the Garden fight, which announces its own Game Controls briefing a second later — in both cases something better arrives immediately. Silence is only correct when something else is already speaking.
- **NEVER run git through the Cowork desktop bridge.** It cannot unlink `.git/index.lock` (`Operation not permitted`), so every command leaves one behind and the NEXT push dies on it -- three times now (2026-08-02, -08-12, -08-15). `push_to_github.bat` Step 0 clears a stale lock when no `git.exe` is running, but the rule is: read repo state from the logs or the GitHub API, never by running git on the mounted folder.
- **GitHub commit history is authoritative** — `github:list_commits` before quoting push state.
- **Position resolvers** (`ResolveLatePositions` → `MatchSet3LateCaptures` → `ResolveStructPositions` → centroid): the first three read live entity structs and silently return ANOTHER entity's data for out-of-window slots. Guard = reject a live read whose triangle ≠ the entity's own static SET3 triangle, in **both** struct-reading passes (`.290` guarded one, `.291` fixed it).
- **Entity-catalog identity:** SYM names are unreliable (kanban2 was Xu) — never expose them, and **never map a SYM to a friendly name because it reads like one** (`.288` mapped `ladline*`→"Ladder" on spelling; `ladline7` was the gate blocking Aaron, hidden 3 BAT cycles). Name only on behavioral evidence (sole entry reaching "In range." at the object; SETMODEL id shared with a known gate). `ResolveFriendlyName()` NEVER returns empty — on a miss it returns the capitalized SYM, so "just remove the row" leaks it; check `ENTITY_DISPLAY_NAMES` explicitly. `ENTITY_SKIP_NAMES` must be checked for JSM-injected objects too, not just Backgrounds. NPC/interaction labels are generic ("NPC N" / "Interaction N") only. Announce-time counters need TYPE-based matching for JSM-injected entries (entityIdx ≤ −300), not the legacy `entityIdx ≥ 0` test.
- **Navigation is screen-relative** (v0.17.0): cardinals map to arrow keys; project through `s_camRight/Down` before `atan2`. **F9 auto-drive uses `s_camRight/Down`** (quantized at load); **chase-drive uses `s_driveCam*`** (CALIB) — don't cross the streams. **F9 corridor steering is OFF** (v0.17.6.2).
- **EWM/battle-menu TTS are load-bearing.** EWM preserves "first-to-fill acts first, no skipped turns, natural ally/enemy ratio". Mechanical splits only unless Aaron approves.
- **Arrival detection / coord capture need the underlying decision VERIFIED, not just signal-presence.** Mid-drive replan must honor the same planner-eligibility gate as initial Start; when "fixing" a planner decline don't substitute a different region (the v0.14.95 mistake).
- **A catalog category can empty mid-visit.** Trigger lines are captured once on field entry; after a battle `[LINE-PAIR] captured=0` and every trigger-line exit disappears (#88). Before blaming a code change for "X vanished", check for a battle and compare against the FIRST field-entry block in the log.
- **Fields have STATE** (`.293`–`.297`, ENABLED). Entities can be alternate world-states of ONE object, gating `SET3` on a shared varblock vs different values; the catalog is built from STATIC data so it would inject every state at once → phantom duplicates. Three-part rule, all three parts load-bearing: **(1)** only a CONDITIONAL entity may be suppressed — a `JPF` (0x02) between the guard read and the `SET3`; without one the `SET3` always runs and the guard feeds a later branch. Not a SYM-name split (glwater2 `saku2` is conditional, `ladline6` is not). **(2)** the group must be ANCHORED — some member's value equals the live byte; otherwise the variable isn't encoding this group's state in a way we understand, so leave it alone (this is what keeps glwater2's three coexisting gates safe). **(3)** then suppress conditional members whose value ≠ live byte. Skips catalog INJECTION only; position data stays intact. A shared varblock does NOT imply mutual exclusion — a naive equality rule would have deleted Gate 3, which the `.295` dry run caught before it shipped.
- **One change per BAT cycle.** If a fix doesn't engage, READ THE LOG first; identical outcomes across "fixes" mean the diagnosis is wrong; when stuck, ask Aaron for one specific observation. His domain knowledge is ground truth but his recipes need verification. **When a fix targets one of several passes writing the same field, check ALL of them.** **Ship a diagnostic, not a guessed fix, when the mapping is inferred** (`.293`).
- **Logs:** `ff8_field.log` is large — bash-grep it. `ff8_nav_data.log` logs every player triangle change. **F11 screenshots are gold** — comparing two shots of the same room across saves is what revealed the fallen-ladder state (`.293`). `[MAPJUMP-HOOK]` = live engine oracle for exit destinations. **`[NAV-PROJ] start ... player=(x,y)` gives Aaron's exact position** — measure catalog entries against it before theorising about what's missing.
